forwards twice while sitting on the ground. 3 -64 

4. Reel 14 — Delete the following shots in the dance by the girl doing snake dance : — 

(a) Shaking her bottom twice ; (b) shaking her breasts in a circular movement ; (c) 

crawling on the floor and shaking the busts. 12 -49 


Total cuts 51 -77 

Actual length of the film after cuts will be— 3851 -76 Metres in 16 Reels.
